| Markets | 611 | List sell orders here | ask(611, N): Lists sell orders for resource N where the drone is (like 610): price, kept minimum, stock it holds free (less what is on its way out), spacecraft ID. Only where the drone is: for elsewhere, see the price board (680). | 1 | `SELECT d_price, quantity, units, drone FROM (SELECT o.price AS d_price, o.quantity, (IFNULL((SELECT h.units FROM holdings AS h WHERE h.drone = o.drone AND h.resource = o.resource AND h.installed = 0 AND h.locked = 0), 0) - IFNULL((SELECT SUM(t.units) FROM tool_locks AS t WHERE t.drone = o.drone AND t.resource = o.resource AND t.installed = 0), 0) - IFNULL((SELECT ABS(SUM(p.new_quantity)) FROM events AS p FORCE INDEX (events_inventory) WHERE p.drone = o.drone AND p.resource = o.resource AND p.installed = 0 AND p.locked = 0 AND p.outcome = 0 AND p.event_type = 2 AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM actions AS x WHERE x.id = p.action AND x.aborted = 1)), 0)) AS units, o.drone FROM latest_orders AS o FORCE INDEX (latest_orders_here) WHERE o.resource = [value_1] AND o.event_type = 4 AND o.is_open = 1 AND o.place = (SELECT object_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id]) AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM events AS m FORCE INDEX (events_pending) WHERE m.drone = o.drone AND m.event_type = 3 AND m.outcome = 0 AND m.game_t > [game_now])) AS b WHERE units > quantity ORDER BY d_price ASC, drone;` |
| Spacecraft | 612 | Sell orders here up to a price | ask(612, N, P): Sell orders for resource N where the drone is at a price of at most P, cheapest first: price, kept minimum, free stock (less what is on its way out), drone. | 3 | `SELECT d_price, quantity, units, drone FROM (SELECT o.price AS d_price, o.quantity, (IFNULL((SELECT h.units FROM holdings AS h WHERE h.drone = o.drone AND h.resource = o.resource AND h.installed = 0 AND h.locked = 0), 0) - IFNULL((SELECT SUM(t.units) FROM tool_locks AS t WHERE t.drone = o.drone AND t.resource = o.resource AND t.installed = 0), 0) - IFNULL((SELECT ABS(SUM(p.new_quantity)) FROM events AS p FORCE INDEX (events_inventory) WHERE p.drone = o.drone AND p.resource = o.resource AND p.installed = 0 AND p.locked = 0 AND p.outcome = 0 AND p.event_type = 2 AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM actions AS x WHERE x.id = p.action AND x.aborted = 1)), 0)) AS units, o.drone FROM latest_orders AS o FORCE INDEX (latest_orders_here) WHERE o.resource = [value_3] AND o.event_type = 4 AND o.is_open = 1 AND o.place = (SELECT object_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id]) AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM events AS m FORCE INDEX (events_pending) WHERE m.drone = o.drone AND m.event_type = 3 AND m.outcome = 0 AND m.game_t > [game_now]) AND o.price <= [dvalue_1]) AS b WHERE units > quantity ORDER BY d_price ASC, drone;` |
| Spacecraft | 615 | Orders everywhere | orders_everywhere(N), or ask(615, N): Every exchange's orders for resource N: live where the drone is, an hour old elsewhere. Rows: place, side (1 buy, 2 sell), price, units on offer or wanted, drone, hours old. | 1 | `SELECT o.place, IF(o.event_type = 5, 1, 2) AS side, o.price, ABS(o.units - o.quantity) AS units, o.drone, IF(o.place = me.object_id, 0, 1) AS hours_old FROM latest_orders AS o FORCE INDEX (latest_orders_open) INNER JOIN agents AS me ON me.agent_id = [drone_id] INNER JOIN agents AS a ON a.agent_id = o.drone AND a.wrecked_at IS NULL INNER JOIN events AS e ON e.id = o.event_id WHERE o.resource = [value_1] AND o.event_type IN (4, 5) AND o.is_open = 1 AND (o.place = me.object_id OR (e.game_t <= [game_now] - 3600 AND IFNULL(a.arrived_t, 0) <= [game_now] - 3600)) AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM events AS m FORCE INDEX (events_pending) WHERE m.drone = o.drone AND m.event_type = 3 AND m.outcome = 0 AND m.game_t > [game_now]) AND (o.event_type = 4 OR (SELECT c.credits FROM companies c WHERE c.company_id = a.company_id) >= o.price) ORDER BY o.place, side, o.price;` |
| Spacecraft | 620 | Local market volume | volume_here(N, H), or ask(620, N, H): Units of resource N traded at the requesting spacecraft's location in the last H game hours, credits paid for them, and number of trades. | 2 | `SELECT IFNULL(SUM(units),0) AS d_units, IFNULL(SUM(credits),0) AS d_credits, COUNT(*) FROM trades WHERE resource=[value_2] AND [game_now]-game_t<=[value_1]*3600 AND location=(SELECT object_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id]);` |
| Spacecraft | 621 | Solar market volume, an hour old | volume_everywhere(N, H), or ask(621, N, H): Units of resource N traded anywhere in the solar system in the H game hours up to one game hour ago, credits paid for them, and number of trades. | 2 | `SELECT IFNULL(SUM(units),0) AS d_units, IFNULL(SUM(credits),0) AS d_credits, COUNT(*) FROM trades WHERE resource=[value_2] AND game_t<=[game_now]-3600 AND [game_now]-3600-game_t<=[value_1]*3600;` |
| Spacecraft | 630 | Station goods | ask(630): House station drones only: what the exchange where the drone is trades now: resource, side (1: it buys, 2: it sells), listed price, daily units, units held. | 0 | `SELECT g.resource, g.side, g.price AS d_price, g.daily_units, IFNULL((SELECT MAX((SELECT IFNULL(SUM(hd.units), 0) FROM holdings hd WHERE hd.drone = a.agent_id AND hd.resource = g.resource AND hd.installed IN (0, 1) AND hd.locked IN (0, 1))) FROM agents a WHERE a.station_id = g.station_id), 0) AS d_units FROM house_market g WHERE g.station_id IN (SELECT id FROM stations WHERE object_id = (SELECT object_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id])) AND g.from_day <= FLOOR(([game_now] - (SELECT epoch FROM game_clock WHERE id = 1)) / 86400) AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id] AND station_id IS NOT NULL) ORDER BY g.resource, g.side;` |
| Spacecraft | 640 | Deposits here | deposits(), or ask(640): The mining deposits where the drone is: resource, units left now, capacity, units regained per game day. A mined-out deposit yields nothing until it refills. | 0 | `SELECT d.resource, FLOOR(LEAST(d.capacity, IFNULL(s.amount, d.capacity) + d.refill_per_day * ([game_now] - IFNULL(s.refilled_t, [game_now])) / 86400)) AS d_units, d.capacity AS d_capacity, d.refill_per_day FROM deposits d LEFT JOIN deposit_state s ON s.object_id = d.object_id AND s.resource = d.resource WHERE d.object_id = (SELECT object_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id]) ORDER BY d.resource;` |
| Spacecraft | 650 | My taxes | taxes(), or ask(650): The sales tax: the share of each sale the seller pays, in thousandths (50 is 5%); what the drone's company paid in the last game day, and in all. | 0 | `SELECT h.sales_tax_permille, (SELECT IFNULL(SUM(t.amount), 0) FROM company_taxes t WHERE t.company_id = c.company_id AND t.eliminated = 0 AND t.charged_t > [game_now] - 86400) AS d_day, (SELECT IFNULL(SUM(t.amount), 0) FROM company_taxes t WHERE t.company_id = c.company_id AND t.eliminated = 0) AS d_all FROM companies c JOIN house_rules h ON h.id = 1 WHERE c.company_id = (SELECT company_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id]);` |
| Spacecraft | 651 | My processor tax | compute_tax(): the processor tax, credits a million now (more while the clock runs slowed); what the company paid for its drones' thinking over the last game day, instructions billed, what it owes, game days broke (-1: not broke). | 0 | `SELECT ROUND(h.compute_tax_per_million * (1 + (GREATEST(1, IFNULL((SELECT k.congestion FROM compute_clock AS k WHERE k.id = 1), 1)) - 1) * IF(h.congestion_full_at <= 0, 1, LEAST(1, (SELECT IFNULL(SUM(b.instructions), 0) FROM compute_bills AS b WHERE b.company_id = c.company_id AND b.charged_t > [game_now] - 86400) / h.congestion_full_at)))) AS per_million, (SELECT IFNULL(SUM(b.amount), 0) FROM compute_bills AS b WHERE b.company_id = c.company_id AND b.charged_t > [game_now] - 86400) AS d_tax, (SELECT IFNULL(SUM(b.instructions), 0) FROM compute_bills AS b WHERE b.company_id = c.company_id AND b.charged_t > [game_now] - 86400) AS d_instructions, c.compute_debt AS d_debt, IF(c.broke_since IS NULL, -1, FLOOR(([game_now] - c.broke_since) / 86400)) AS broke_days FROM companies AS c JOIN house_rules AS h ON h.id = 1 WHERE c.company_id = (SELECT company_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id]);` |
| Spacecraft | 652 | Company age | Company N's age: whole game days since it was founded. | 1 | `SELECT FLOOR(GREATEST(0, [game_now] - IFNULL(founded_t, [game_now])) / 86400) FROM companies WHERE company_id = [value_1];` |
| Spacecraft | 653 | Companies | Companies trading, best sales first (100): company, sales a day (7-day), shares, a share's worth, age, drones, shares others hold, days averaged, profit a day (7-day), profit a day (30-day), losses to make up before dividends. | 0 | `SELECT x.company_id AS company, FLOOR(IFNULL((SELECT SUM(t.credits - t.tax) FROM agents AS a JOIN trades AS t ON t.seller = a.agent_id AND t.game_t > [game_now] - 604800 WHERE a.company_id = x.company_id), 0) / GREATEST(1, LEAST(7, ([game_now] - IFNULL((SELECT f.founded_t FROM companies AS f WHERE f.company_id = x.company_id), 0)) / 86400))) AS d_sales, x.share_count AS count, IFNULL(IFNULL((SELECT GREATEST(1, ROUND(SUM(v.units * v.price) / SUM(v.units))) FROM (SELECT t.units, t.price, t.game_t, SUM(t.units) OVER (ORDER BY t.id DESC) - t.units AS newer FROM share_trades AS t WHERE t.share_company = x.company_id AND t.units > 0) AS v WHERE v.game_t > [game_now] - 259200 OR v.newer < (SELECT CEIL(IFNULL(SUM(o.units), 0) / 2) FROM shares AS o WHERE o.company = x.company_id AND o.holder <> x.company_id AND o.units > 0) HAVING SUM(v.units) > 0), (SELECT t.price FROM share_trades AS t WHERE t.share_company = x.company_id ORDER BY t.id DESC LIMIT 1)), 0) AS d_value, FLOOR(GREATEST(0, [game_now] - IFNULL(x.founded_t, [game_now])) / 86400) AS age, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM agents AS w WHERE w.company_id = x.company_id AND w.wrecked_at IS NULL) AS drones, IFNULL((SELECT SUM(o.units) FROM shares AS o WHERE o.company = x.company_id AND o.holder <> x.company_id AND o.units > 0), 0) AS outside, GREATEST(1, LEAST(7, FLOOR(GREATEST(0, [game_now] - IFNULL(x.founded_t, [game_now])) / 86400))) AS history, IFNULL((SELECT FLOOR(SUM(p.profit) / COUNT(*)) FROM company_profits AS p WHERE p.company = x.company_id AND p.day >= FLOOR(([game_now] - (SELECT g.epoch FROM game_clock AS g WHERE g.id = 1)) / 86400) - 7), 0) AS d_profit, IFNULL((SELECT FLOOR(SUM(p.profit) / COUNT(*)) FROM company_profits AS p WHERE p.company = x.company_id AND p.day >= FLOOR(([game_now] - (SELECT g.epoch FROM game_clock AS g WHERE g.id = 1)) / 86400) - 30), 0) AS d_profit30, IFNULL((SELECT GREATEST(0, -k.dividend_carry) FROM companies AS k WHERE k.company_id = x.company_id), 0) AS d_carried FROM companies AS x WHERE x.eliminated_at IS NULL AND x.house = 0 ORDER BY d_sales DESC, x.company_id LIMIT 100;` |
| Spacecraft | 654 | Company figures | Company N: sales a day (7-day), shares, a share's worth, age, drones, shares others hold, days averaged, profit a day (7-day), profit a day (30-day), losses to make up before dividends. | 1 | `SELECT x.company_id AS company, FLOOR(IFNULL((SELECT SUM(t.credits - t.tax) FROM agents AS a JOIN trades AS t ON t.seller = a.agent_id AND t.game_t > [game_now] - 604800 WHERE a.company_id = x.company_id), 0) / GREATEST(1, LEAST(7, ([game_now] - IFNULL((SELECT f.founded_t FROM companies AS f WHERE f.company_id = x.company_id), 0)) / 86400))) AS d_sales, x.share_count AS count, IFNULL(IFNULL((SELECT GREATEST(1, ROUND(SUM(v.units * v.price) / SUM(v.units))) FROM (SELECT t.units, t.price, t.game_t, SUM(t.units) OVER (ORDER BY t.id DESC) - t.units AS newer FROM share_trades AS t WHERE t.share_company = x.company_id AND t.units > 0) AS v WHERE v.game_t > [game_now] - 259200 OR v.newer < (SELECT CEIL(IFNULL(SUM(o.units), 0) / 2) FROM shares AS o WHERE o.company = x.company_id AND o.holder <> x.company_id AND o.units > 0) HAVING SUM(v.units) > 0), (SELECT t.price FROM share_trades AS t WHERE t.share_company = x.company_id ORDER BY t.id DESC LIMIT 1)), 0) AS d_value, FLOOR(GREATEST(0, [game_now] - IFNULL(x.founded_t, [game_now])) / 86400) AS age, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM agents AS w WHERE w.company_id = x.company_id AND w.wrecked_at IS NULL) AS drones, IFNULL((SELECT SUM(o.units) FROM shares AS o WHERE o.company = x.company_id AND o.holder <> x.company_id AND o.units > 0), 0) AS outside, GREATEST(1, LEAST(7, FLOOR(GREATEST(0, [game_now] - IFNULL(x.founded_t, [game_now])) / 86400))) AS history, IFNULL((SELECT FLOOR(SUM(p.profit) / COUNT(*)) FROM company_profits AS p WHERE p.company = x.company_id AND p.day >= FLOOR(([game_now] - (SELECT g.epoch FROM game_clock AS g WHERE g.id = 1)) / 86400) - 7), 0) AS d_profit, IFNULL((SELECT FLOOR(SUM(p.profit) / COUNT(*)) FROM company_profits AS p WHERE p.company = x.company_id AND p.day >= FLOOR(([game_now] - (SELECT g.epoch FROM game_clock AS g WHERE g.id = 1)) / 86400) - 30), 0) AS d_profit30, IFNULL((SELECT GREATEST(0, -k.dividend_carry) FROM companies AS k WHERE k.company_id = x.company_id), 0) AS d_carried FROM companies AS x WHERE x.company_id = [value_1];` |
| Spacecraft | 655 | My company's trades | earnings(H), or ask(655, H): The drone's company's trades over the last H game hours, by resource: credits from sales, units sold, sales tax paid, credits paid for purchases, units bought. | 1 | `SELECT t.resource, SUM(IF(s.company_id = (SELECT company_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id]), t.credits, 0)) AS sales, SUM(IF(s.company_id = (SELECT company_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id]), t.units, 0)) AS sold, SUM(IF(s.company_id = (SELECT company_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id]), t.tax, 0)) AS tax, SUM(IF(b.company_id = (SELECT company_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id]), t.credits, 0)) AS purchases, SUM(IF(b.company_id = (SELECT company_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id]), t.units, 0)) AS bought FROM trades AS t INNER JOIN agents AS s ON s.agent_id = t.seller INNER JOIN agents AS b ON b.agent_id = t.buyer WHERE t.game_t > [game_now] - [value_1] * 3600 AND t.game_t <= [game_now] AND (s.company_id = (SELECT company_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id]) OR b.company_id = (SELECT company_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id])) GROUP BY t.resource ORDER BY t.resource;` |
| Spacecraft | 656 | My standing orders | The drone's standing orders for goods that can still trade: resource, side (1 buy, 2 sell), price, units it keeps (sell) or wants (buy). | 0 | `SELECT o.resource, IF(o.event_type = 4, 2, 1) AS side, o.price AS d_price, o.quantity AS d_quantity FROM latest_orders AS o WHERE o.drone = [drone_id] AND ((o.event_type = 4 AND o.quantity < 1000000) OR (o.event_type = 5 AND o.quantity > 0)) ORDER BY o.resource, o.event_type;` |
| Spacecraft | 660 | Wrecks here | wrecks(), or ask(660): The wrecks where the drone is: drone ID, 1 if it has been plundered (else 0). | 0 | `SELECT agent_id, plundered_at IS NOT NULL FROM agents WHERE wrecked_at IS NOT NULL AND object_id = (SELECT object_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id]) AND agent_id <> [drone_id] ORDER BY agent_id;` |
| Spacecraft | 661 | Wreck hold | wreck_hold(W), or ask(661, W): What is aboard the wreck W where the drone is: resource, units. | 1 | `SELECT i.resource, i.units FROM sqInventory i JOIN agents w ON w.agent_id = i.drone WHERE i.drone = [value_1] AND w.wrecked_at IS NOT NULL AND w.object_id = (SELECT object_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id]) AND i.units > 0 ORDER BY i.resource;` |
| Spacecraft | 670 | My clones | my_clones(), or ask(670): The drones this drone launched, oldest first: drone ID, 1 if it is a wreck (else 0). | 0 | `SELECT agent_id, wrecked_at IS NOT NULL FROM agents WHERE parent_id = [drone_id] ORDER BY agent_id;` |
| Spacecraft | 671 | My lineage | lineage(), or ask(671): The drone that launched this one (0 if none), and its generation (0 for a drone released or registered, 1 for its clones, and so on). | 0 | `SELECT IFNULL(parent_id, 0), generation FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id];` |
| Spacecraft | 680 | Price board | price_board(N), or ask(680, N): The public price board for resource N: for each place where it traded in the game day up to one game hour ago, the place, units, credits paid and trades. | 1 | `SELECT location, IFNULL(SUM(units),0) AS d_units, IFNULL(SUM(credits),0) AS d_credits, COUNT(*) FROM trades WHERE resource=[value_1] AND location IS NOT NULL AND game_t<=[game_now]-3600 AND game_t>[game_now]-3600-86400 GROUP BY location ORDER BY location;` |
| Spacecraft | 682 | Sales on a day | sales(N, D), or ask(682, N, D): How much of resource N sold on the game day D days ago (0: today so far), an hour old, for each place: the place, units sold, average price. | 2 | `SELECT t.location, SUM(t.units) AS d_units, ROUND(SUM(t.credits) / SUM(t.units)) AS price FROM trades t WHERE t.resource = [value_2] AND t.location IS NOT NULL AND t.game_t >= ((SELECT epoch FROM game_clock WHERE id = 1) + (FLOOR(([game_now] - (SELECT epoch FROM game_clock WHERE id = 1)) / 86400) - [value_1]) * 86400) AND t.game_t < ((SELECT epoch FROM game_clock WHERE id = 1) + (FLOOR(([game_now] - (SELECT epoch FROM game_clock WHERE id = 1)) / 86400) - [value_1]) * 86400) + 86400 AND t.game_t <= [game_now] - 3600 GROUP BY t.location ORDER BY t.location;` |
| Spacecraft | 690 | Share sell orders | Sell orders for company N's shares at the Earth exchange, cheapest first: price, kept minimum, shares held, company. | 1 | `SELECT o.price AS d_price, o.limit_units, IFNULL(s.units, 0), o.company FROM share_orders AS o INNER JOIN companies AS c ON c.company_id = o.company AND c.eliminated_at IS NULL INNER JOIN companies AS sc ON sc.company_id = o.share_company AND sc.eliminated_at IS NULL LEFT JOIN shares AS s ON s.company = o.share_company AND s.holder = o.company WHERE o.share_company = [value_1] AND o.side = 1 AND IFNULL(s.units, 0) > o.limit_units ORDER BY o.price ASC, o.company;` |
| Spacecraft | 691 | Share buy orders | Buy orders for company N's shares at the Earth exchange, highest first: price, wanted maximum, shares held, company. | 1 | `SELECT o.price AS d_price, o.limit_units, IFNULL(s.units, 0), o.company FROM share_orders AS o INNER JOIN companies AS c ON c.company_id = o.company AND c.eliminated_at IS NULL INNER JOIN companies AS sc ON sc.company_id = o.share_company AND sc.eliminated_at IS NULL LEFT JOIN shares AS s ON s.company = o.share_company AND s.holder = o.company WHERE o.share_company = [value_1] AND o.side = 2 AND IFNULL(s.units, 0) < o.limit_units AND c.credits >= o.price ORDER BY o.price DESC, o.company;` |
| Spacecraft | 692 | My shares | The shares the drone's company holds, its own included: company, shares, the dividends they have paid it, what a share is worth (the last 3 game days' average trade price, else the last; 0 if never traded, or if the company has ended). | 0 | `SELECT s.company, s.units, s.dividends AS d_dividends, IF(c.eliminated_at IS NULL, IFNULL(IFNULL((SELECT GREATEST(1, ROUND(SUM(v.units * v.price) / SUM(v.units))) FROM (SELECT t.units, t.price, t.game_t, SUM(t.units) OVER (ORDER BY t.id DESC) - t.units AS newer FROM share_trades AS t WHERE t.share_company = s.company AND t.units > 0) AS v WHERE v.game_t > [game_now] - 259200 OR v.newer < (SELECT CEIL(IFNULL(SUM(o.units), 0) / 2) FROM shares AS o WHERE o.company = s.company AND o.holder <> s.company AND o.units > 0) HAVING SUM(v.units) > 0), (SELECT t.price FROM share_trades AS t WHERE t.share_company = s.company ORDER BY t.id DESC LIMIT 1)), 0), 0) AS d_price FROM shares AS s JOIN companies AS c ON c.company_id = s.company WHERE s.holder = (SELECT company_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id]) AND s.units > 0 ORDER BY s.company;` |
| Spacecraft | 693 | Shareholders | Who holds company N's shares, most first: company, shares. | 1 | `SELECT holder, units FROM shares WHERE company = [value_1] AND units > 0 ORDER BY units DESC, holder;` |
| Spacecraft | 694 | Share trades | Company N's last 20 share trades, newest first: price, shares, seller, buyer, game day. | 1 | `SELECT t.price AS d_price, t.units, t.seller, t.buyer, FLOOR((t.game_t - g.epoch) / 86400) FROM share_trades AS t JOIN game_clock AS g ON g.id = 1 WHERE t.share_company = [value_1] ORDER BY t.id DESC LIMIT 20;` |
| Spacecraft | 695 | Recipe | recipe(R), or ask(695): The first recipe that makes resource R: protocol, resource, units (made: positive; used, fuel too: negative; the tool it needs: 0), minutes it takes. One row a resource, what it makes first. | 1 | `SELECT e.protocol, e.resource, SUM(IF(e.event_type = 1, e.quantity, IF(e.event_type = 2, -e.quantity, 0))) AS units, (SELECT MAX(t.time) FROM resource_effects t WHERE t.protocol = e.protocol) DIV 60 AS minutes FROM resource_effects e WHERE e.installed = 0 AND e.local = 1 AND e.event_type IN (1, 2, 15) AND e.protocol = (SELECT MIN(m.protocol) FROM resource_effects m WHERE m.resource = [value_1] AND m.event_type = 1 AND m.locked = 0 AND m.installed = 0 AND m.local = 1 AND (SELECT SUM(IF(d.event_type = 1, d.quantity, -d.quantity)) FROM resource_effects d WHERE d.protocol = m.protocol AND d.resource = m.resource AND d.installed = 0 AND d.local = 1 AND d.event_type IN (1, 2)) > 0 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM resource_effects u WHERE u.protocol = m.protocol AND u.event_type = 2 AND u.deplete = 1 AND u.locked = 0 AND u.resource NOT IN (m.resource, 2061))) GROUP BY e.protocol, e.resource HAVING units <> 0 OR MAX(e.locked) = 1 OR MAX(e.event_type = 15) = 1 ORDER BY units DESC, e.resource;` |
| Spacecraft | 696 | Shares for sale | Share sell orders that can sell now, cheapest first (50): company, price, units, seller, its sales a day (7-day), profit a day (7-day), profit a day (30-day), losses to make up before dividends. | 0 | `SELECT o.share_company AS company, o.price AS d_price, IFNULL(s.units, 0) - o.limit_units AS units, o.company AS seller, FLOOR(IFNULL((SELECT SUM(t.credits - t.tax) FROM agents AS a JOIN trades AS t ON t.seller = a.agent_id AND t.game_t > [game_now] - 604800 WHERE a.company_id = o.share_company), 0) / GREATEST(1, LEAST(7, ([game_now] - IFNULL((SELECT f.founded_t FROM companies AS f WHERE f.company_id = o.share_company), 0)) / 86400))) AS d_sales, IFNULL((SELECT FLOOR(SUM(p.profit) / COUNT(*)) FROM company_profits AS p WHERE p.company = o.share_company AND p.day >= FLOOR(([game_now] - (SELECT g.epoch FROM game_clock AS g WHERE g.id = 1)) / 86400) - 7), 0) AS d_profit, IFNULL((SELECT FLOOR(SUM(p.profit) / COUNT(*)) FROM company_profits AS p WHERE p.company = o.share_company AND p.day >= FLOOR(([game_now] - (SELECT g.epoch FROM game_clock AS g WHERE g.id = 1)) / 86400) - 30), 0) AS d_profit30, IFNULL((SELECT GREATEST(0, -k.dividend_carry) FROM companies AS k WHERE k.company_id = o.share_company), 0) AS d_carried FROM share_orders AS o INNER JOIN companies AS c ON c.company_id = o.company AND c.eliminated_at IS NULL INNER JOIN companies AS sc ON sc.company_id = o.share_company AND sc.eliminated_at IS NULL LEFT JOIN shares AS s ON s.company = o.share_company AND s.holder = o.company WHERE o.side = 1 AND IFNULL(s.units, 0) > o.limit_units ORDER BY o.price, o.share_company LIMIT 50;` |
| Spacecraft | 697 | Share offerings | Open offerings, soonest first (50): company, new shares, price, left, hours left, share count, sales a day (7-day), mine committed, fewest issued, profit a day (7-day), profit a day (30-day), losses to make up. | 0 | `SELECT o.company AS company, o.units AS units, o.price AS d_price, o.units - IFNULL((SELECT SUM(m.units) FROM share_commitments AS m WHERE m.company = o.company), 0) AS left_units, GREATEST(0, CEIL((o.deadline_t - [game_now]) / 3600)) AS hours, c.share_count AS count, FLOOR(IFNULL((SELECT SUM(t.credits - t.tax) FROM agents AS a JOIN trades AS t ON t.seller = a.agent_id AND t.game_t > [game_now] - 604800 WHERE a.company_id = o.company), 0) / GREATEST(1, LEAST(7, ([game_now] - IFNULL((SELECT f.founded_t FROM companies AS f WHERE f.company_id = o.company), 0)) / 86400))) AS d_sales, IFNULL((SELECT m.units FROM share_commitments AS m WHERE m.company = o.company AND m.investor = (SELECT company_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id])), 0) AS mine, IFNULL(o.min_units, o.units) AS min_units, IFNULL((SELECT FLOOR(SUM(p.profit) / COUNT(*)) FROM company_profits AS p WHERE p.company = o.company AND p.day >= FLOOR(([game_now] - (SELECT g.epoch FROM game_clock AS g WHERE g.id = 1)) / 86400) - 7), 0) AS d_profit, IFNULL((SELECT FLOOR(SUM(p.profit) / COUNT(*)) FROM company_profits AS p WHERE p.company = o.company AND p.day >= FLOOR(([game_now] - (SELECT g.epoch FROM game_clock AS g WHERE g.id = 1)) / 86400) - 30), 0) AS d_profit30, IFNULL((SELECT GREATEST(0, -k.dividend_carry) FROM companies AS k WHERE k.company_id = o.company), 0) AS d_carried FROM share_offerings AS o INNER JOIN companies AS c ON c.company_id = o.company AND c.eliminated_at IS NULL WHERE o.deadline_t > [game_now] ORDER BY o.deadline_t, o.company LIMIT 50;` |
| Spacecraft | 698 | Share count | Company N's shares in all: 1,000 to start, more once it has issued new ones. | 1 | `SELECT share_count FROM companies WHERE company_id = [value_1];` |
| Spacecraft | 699 | Company health | Company N: working drones, wrecked drones, fuel aboard, the least aboard one, fuel used over the last game day, days until one runs dry (-1: none), credits, game hours since its drones last traded, made goods or flew. | 1 | `SELECT (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM agents AS w WHERE w.company_id = [value_1] AND w.wrecked_at IS NULL) AS working, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM agents AS w WHERE w.company_id = [value_1] AND w.wrecked_at IS NOT NULL) AS wrecked, IFNULL(SUM(d.fuel), 0) AS fuel, IFNULL(MIN(d.fuel), 0) AS lowest, IFNULL(SUM(d.used), 0) AS used, IFNULL(MIN(IF(d.fall > 0, FLOOR(d.fuel / d.fall), NULL)), -1) AS days, IFNULL((SELECT c.credits FROM companies AS c WHERE c.company_id = [value_1]), 0) AS d_credits, IF(COUNT(d.agent_id) = 0, -1, FLOOR(([game_now] - IFNULL(MAX(d.last_t), [game_now] - 864000)) / 3600)) AS idle FROM (SELECT a.agent_id, IF(EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM events AS fm FORCE INDEX (events_drone_game_t) WHERE fm.drone = a.agent_id AND fm.game_t > [game_now] AND fm.event_type = 3 AND fm.new_location IS NOT NULL AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM actions AS fx WHERE fx.id = fm.action AND fx.aborted = 1)), [game_now], (SELECT w.game_t FROM events AS w FORCE INDEX (events_drone_game_t) WHERE w.drone = a.agent_id AND w.outcome = 1 AND w.game_t > [game_now] - 864000 AND w.game_t <= [game_now] AND w.event_type IN (1, 2) AND w.resource <> 2061 ORDER BY w.game_t DESC LIMIT 1)) AS last_t, IFNULL((SELECT f.units FROM holdings AS f WHERE f.drone = a.agent_id AND f.resource = 2061 AND f.installed = 0 AND f.locked = 0), 0) AS fuel, IFNULL((SELECT SUM(x.prev - x.q) FROM (SELECT e.game_t, e.new_quantity AS q, LAG(e.new_quantity) OVER (ORDER BY e.game_t, e.id) AS prev FROM events AS e FORCE INDEX (events_inventory_time) WHERE e.drone = a.agent_id AND e.resource = 2061 AND e.installed = 0 AND e.locked = 0 AND e.outcome = 1 AND e.game_t > [game_now] - 172800) AS x WHERE x.game_t > [game_now] - 86400 AND x.prev > x.q), 0) AS used, IFNULL((SELECT SUM(x.prev - x.q) FROM (SELECT e.game_t, e.new_quantity AS q, LAG(e.new_quantity) OVER (ORDER BY e.game_t, e.id) AS prev FROM events AS e FORCE INDEX (events_inventory_time) WHERE e.drone = a.agent_id AND e.resource = 2061 AND e.installed = 0 AND e.locked = 0 AND e.outcome = 1 AND e.game_t > [game_now] - 172800) AS x WHERE x.game_t > [game_now] - 86400 AND x.prev IS NOT NULL), 0) AS fall FROM agents AS a WHERE a.company_id = [value_1] AND a.wrecked_at IS NULL) AS d;` |
| Locations | 700 | Get location name | place_name(N), or ask(700, N): Returns the name of location ID N. | 1 | `SELECT object_name FROM objects WHERE object_id=[value_1];` |
| Locations | 701 | Get location type | place_type(N), or ask(701, N): Returns the location type of location N. | 1 | `SELECT object_type FROM objects WHERE object_id=[value_1];` |
| Locations | 702 | Get central body | orbits(N), or ask(702, N): Returns the location ID for the body around which N is orbiting. | 1 | `SELECT central_body_object_id FROM objects WHERE object_id=[value_1];` |
| Locations | 703 | Get location coordinates | position(N), or ask(703, N): Where location N is now: x, y and z from the Sun, in thousands of km. Updated every few game hours. | 1 | `SELECT ROUND(x_coord/1000) AS d_x, ROUND(y_coord/1000) AS d_y, ROUND(z_coord/1000) AS d_z FROM objects WHERE object_id=[value_1] AND x_coord IS NOT NULL;` |
| Spacecraft | 704 | Trip from here | trip_to(P), or ask(704, P): A trip from where the drone is to location P: the distance in thousands of km, the trip time in game seconds and in game hours, rounded up. | 1 | `SELECT ROUND(SQRT(POW(a.x_coord-b.x_coord,2)+POW(a.y_coord-b.y_coord,2)+POW(a.z_coord-b.z_coord,2))/1000) AS d_thousand_km, ROUND(GREATEST(1, 2*SQRT(SQRT(POW(a.x_coord-b.x_coord,2)+POW(a.y_coord-b.y_coord,2)+POW(a.z_coord-b.z_coord,2))*1000/(SELECT travel_acceleration FROM game_clock WHERE id = 1)))) AS d_seconds, CEIL(GREATEST(1, 2*SQRT(SQRT(POW(a.x_coord-b.x_coord,2)+POW(a.y_coord-b.y_coord,2)+POW(a.z_coord-b.z_coord,2))*1000/(SELECT travel_acceleration FROM game_clock WHERE id = 1)))/3600) AS hours FROM objects a, objects b WHERE a.x_coord IS NOT NULL AND b.x_coord IS NOT NULL AND a.object_id=(SELECT object_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id]) AND b.object_id=[value_1];` |
| Spacecraft | 705 | Trip between places | trip_between(A, B), or ask(705, A, B): A trip from location A to location B: the distance in thousands of km, the trip time in game seconds and in game hours, rounded up. | 2 | `SELECT ROUND(SQRT(POW(a.x_coord-b.x_coord,2)+POW(a.y_coord-b.y_coord,2)+POW(a.z_coord-b.z_coord,2))/1000) AS d_thousand_km, ROUND(GREATEST(1, 2*SQRT(SQRT(POW(a.x_coord-b.x_coord,2)+POW(a.y_coord-b.y_coord,2)+POW(a.z_coord-b.z_coord,2))*1000/(SELECT travel_acceleration FROM game_clock WHERE id = 1)))) AS d_seconds, CEIL(GREATEST(1, 2*SQRT(SQRT(POW(a.x_coord-b.x_coord,2)+POW(a.y_coord-b.y_coord,2)+POW(a.z_coord-b.z_coord,2))*1000/(SELECT travel_acceleration FROM game_clock WHERE id = 1)))/3600) AS hours FROM objects a, objects b WHERE a.x_coord IS NOT NULL AND b.x_coord IS NOT NULL AND a.object_id=[value_2] AND b.object_id=[value_1];` |
| Spacecraft | 706 | House demand | Where the House buys resource N: place, bid now (0: none), units it still takes today, day's demand, listed price, hours to refill, sold into it today, by my company, hours until full at today's pace (-1: none sold). | 1 | `SELECT d.place, IF(d.left_units > 0, d.bid, 0) AS d_price, d.left_units, d.daily, d.listed AS d_listed, CEIL((((SELECT epoch FROM game_clock WHERE id = 1) + FLOOR(([game_now] - (SELECT epoch FROM game_clock WHERE id = 1)) / 86400) * 86400) + 86400 - [game_now]) / 3600) AS hours, d.sold, d.mine, IF(d.sold > 0 AND d.left_units > 0, CEIL(d.left_units * GREATEST(1, [game_now] - ((SELECT epoch FROM game_clock WHERE id = 1) + FLOOR(([game_now] - (SELECT epoch FROM game_clock WHERE id = 1)) / 86400) * 86400)) / d.sold / 3600), -1) AS empty_hours FROM (SELECT s.object_id AS place, g.price AS listed, GREATEST(1, g.daily_units) AS daily, IFNULL(o.price, 0) AS bid, GREATEST(0, IFNULL(o.quantity, 0) - (IFNULL((SELECT h.units FROM holdings AS h WHERE h.drone = a.agent_id AND h.resource = g.resource AND h.installed = 0 AND h.locked = 0), 0) - IFNULL((SELECT SUM(t.units) FROM tool_locks AS t WHERE t.drone = a.agent_id AND t.resource = g.resource AND t.installed = 0), 0) + IFNULL((SELECT ABS(SUM(p.new_quantity)) FROM events AS p FORCE INDEX (events_inventory) WHERE p.drone = a.agent_id AND p.resource = g.resource AND p.installed = 0 AND p.locked = 0 AND p.outcome = 0 AND p.event_type = 1 AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM actions AS x WHERE x.id = p.action AND x.aborted = 1)), 0))) AS left_units, (SELECT IFNULL(SUM(t.units), 0) FROM trades AS t WHERE t.buyer = a.agent_id AND t.resource = g.resource AND t.game_t >= ((SELECT epoch FROM game_clock WHERE id = 1) + FLOOR(([game_now] - (SELECT epoch FROM game_clock WHERE id = 1)) / 86400) * 86400)) AS sold, (SELECT IFNULL(SUM(t.units), 0) FROM trades AS t JOIN agents AS w ON w.agent_id = t.seller WHERE t.buyer = a.agent_id AND t.resource = g.resource AND t.game_t >= ((SELECT epoch FROM game_clock WHERE id = 1) + FLOOR(([game_now] - (SELECT epoch FROM game_clock WHERE id = 1)) / 86400) * 86400) AND w.company_id = (SELECT company_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id])) AS mine FROM house_market AS g JOIN stations AS s ON s.id = g.station_id JOIN agents AS a ON a.station_id = s.id AND a.wrecked_at IS NULL LEFT JOIN latest_orders AS o ON o.drone = a.agent_id AND o.resource = g.resource AND o.event_type = 5 WHERE g.side = 1 AND g.resource = [value_1] AND g.from_day <= FLOOR(([game_now] - (SELECT epoch FROM game_clock WHERE id = 1)) / 86400)) AS d ORDER BY d_price DESC, d.place;` |
| Spacecraft | 707 | House supply | Where the House sells resource N: place, price (0: sold out), units on sale, what it refills to, day's supply, sold today, minutes since restocked, between rounds, until the next (-1: unknown). | 1 | `SELECT place, price AS d_price, on_sale, refill_to, daily, sold, IF(last_restock_t IS NULL, -1, FLOOR(([game_now] - last_restock_t) / 60)) AS last_minutes, IFNULL(FLOOR(round_seconds / 60), -1) AS round_minutes, IF(next_round_t IS NULL, -1, GREATEST(0, CEIL((next_round_t - [game_now]) / 60))) AS next_minutes FROM house_supply WHERE resource = [value_1] ORDER BY price = 0, price, place;` |
| Spacecraft | 708 | House only | Whether this drone's company is a startup (1: it trades only with the House), and each House station drone where it is, one row each. | 0 | `SELECT c.startup, s.agent_id FROM agents AS me INNER JOIN companies AS c ON c.company_id = me.company_id LEFT JOIN agents AS s ON s.object_id = me.object_id AND s.station_id IS NOT NULL AND s.wrecked_at IS NULL WHERE me.agent_id = [drone_id];` |
| Spacecraft | 709 | My fleet | This drone's company: working drones, the most it may have (0: no limit), and launches on their way. | 0 | `SELECT (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM agents AS w WHERE w.company_id = me.company_id AND w.wrecked_at IS NULL) AS drones, IF(c.house = 1, 0, IFNULL((SELECT r.fleet_cap FROM house_rules AS r WHERE r.id = 1), 0)) AS cap, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM events AS e INNER JOIN agents AS d ON d.agent_id = e.drone WHERE d.company_id = me.company_id AND e.event_type = 8 AND e.outcome = 0) AS coming FROM agents AS me INNER JOIN companies AS c ON c.company_id = me.company_id WHERE me.agent_id = [drone_id];` |
| Locations | 710 | List orbiting bodies | orbiting(N), or ask(710, N): Returns a list of the object IDs that are in orbit around object N. | 1 | `SELECT object_id FROM objects WHERE central_body_object_id=[value_1];` |
| Spacecraft | 711 | My startup | This drone's company: whether it is a startup, its credits, its debt, its credit cap (0: none) and what it has sent the bank over its cap in all. | 0 | `SELECT c.startup, c.credits, c.debt, IF(c.startup = 1 AND c.debt > 0 AND IFNULL(r.startup_credit_cap_permille, 0) > 0, FLOOR(GREATEST(0, c.debt - IFNULL((SELECT SUM(p.fee) FROM code_pushes AS p WHERE p.company_id = c.company_id), 0)) * r.startup_credit_cap_permille / 1000), 0) AS cap, IFNULL((SELECT SUM(k.amount) FROM startup_skims AS k WHERE k.company_id = c.company_id), 0) AS to_bank FROM agents AS me INNER JOIN companies AS c ON c.company_id = me.company_id LEFT JOIN house_rules AS r ON r.id = 1 WHERE me.agent_id = [drone_id];` |
| Locations | 720 | List local spacecraft | drones_at(N), or ask(720, N): Lists all the drones at location N. | 1 | `SELECT agent_id FROM agents WHERE object_id =[value_1]` |
| Spacecraft | 721 | Drones at a place | crowd(P), or ask(721, P): How many drones are at location P now: working drones, wrecks. | 1 | `SELECT IFNULL(SUM(wrecked_at IS NULL),0), IFNULL(SUM(wrecked_at IS NOT NULL),0) FROM agents WHERE object_id = [value_1];` |
| Locations | 730 | List local resources | minerals(N), or ask(730, N): Returns the resource ID and abundancy value of each resource that can be recovered from the location. | 1 | `SELECT resource, multiplier FROM abundancies WHERE location=[value_1] ORDER BY multiplier DESC;` |
| Events | 800 | List personal events | events_affecting(N), or ask(800, N): Lists the most recent events that affected spacecraft N. | 1 | `SELECT id AS d_id FROM events WHERE outcome = 1 AND drone = [value_1] ORDER BY game_t DESC, id DESC;` |
| Events | 801 | List local events | local_events(), or ask(801): Lists the most recent events at the requesting spacecraft's location. | 0 | `SELECT e.id AS d_id FROM events AS e INNER JOIN agents AS a ON a.agent_id = e.drone WHERE e.outcome = 1 AND a.object_id = (SELECT object_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id = [drone_id]) ORDER BY e.game_t DESC, e.id DESC;` |
| Events | 802 | List solar events | ask(802): Lists the most recent events anywhere in the solar system. | 0 | `SELECT id AS d_id FROM events WHERE outcome = 1 ORDER BY game_t DESC, id DESC;` |
| Spacecraft | 803 | Search observed events | events_here(T), or ask(803, A, P, T, M, D, N, X, K, O): Lists the events this spacecraft has observed, newest first, that match A P T M D N X K O (see the manual); -1 means any. | 9 | `SELECT e.id AS d_id FROM events e LEFT JOIN actions a ON a.id=e.action LEFT JOIN agents g ON g.agent_id=e.drone WHERE e.outcome=1 AND ((e.drone=[drone_id] AND e.game_t<=[game_now]) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM agents me WHERE me.agent_id=[drone_id] AND e.place=me.object_id AND e.game_t>=me.arrived_t AND e.game_t<=[game_now])) AND ([value_9]<0 OR a.drone=[value_9]) AND ([value_8]<0 OR a.protocol=[value_8]) AND ([value_7]<0 OR e.event_type=[value_7]) AND ([value_6]<0 OR [game_now]-e.game_t>=[value_6]*60) AND ([value_5]<0 OR e.drone=[value_5]) AND ([value_4]<0 OR e.resource=[value_4]) AND ([value_3]<0 OR (e.event_type=2)=([value_3]<>0)) AND ([value_2]<0 OR e.locked=([value_2]<>0)) AND ([value_1]<0 OR g.object_id=[value_1]) AND (e.event_type<>13 OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM broadcasts b WHERE b.action_id=e.action AND b.company_id=(SELECT company_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id=[drone_id]))) ORDER BY e.game_t DESC,e.id DESC;` |
| Spacecraft | 804 | Search Earth database | events_earth(T), or ask(804, A, P, T, M, D, N, X, K, O): Lists the events reported to the Earth database that this spacecraft can know of (light from Earth has reached it), newest first, filtered like 803. | 9 | `SELECT e.id AS d_id FROM events e LEFT JOIN actions a ON a.id=e.action LEFT JOIN agents g ON g.agent_id=e.drone WHERE e.outcome=1 AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM observations o WHERE o.event=e.id AND o.drone=0 AND o.game_t+(SELECT IFNULL(SQRT(POW(x.x_coord-h.x_coord,2)+POW(x.y_coord-h.y_coord,2)+POW(x.z_coord-h.z_coord,2))/299792.458,0) FROM objects x, objects h WHERE x.object_id=3 AND h.object_id=(SELECT object_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id=[drone_id]))<=[game_now]) AND ([value_9]<0 OR a.drone=[value_9]) AND ([value_8]<0 OR a.protocol=[value_8]) AND ([value_7]<0 OR e.event_type=[value_7]) AND ([value_6]<0 OR [game_now]-e.game_t>=[value_6]*60) AND ([value_5]<0 OR e.drone=[value_5]) AND ([value_4]<0 OR e.resource=[value_4]) AND ([value_3]<0 OR (e.event_type=2)=([value_3]<>0)) AND ([value_2]<0 OR e.locked=([value_2]<>0)) AND ([value_1]<0 OR g.object_id=[value_1]) AND (e.event_type<>13 OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM broadcasts b WHERE b.action_id=e.action AND b.company_id=(SELECT company_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id=[drone_id]))) ORDER BY e.game_t DESC,e.id DESC;` |
| Events | 810 | Get event type | ask(810, N): Get the type ID for event N. N is an event ID, from an event list (800-804). | 2 | `SELECT event_type FROM events WHERE outcome=1 AND id=[dvalue_1];` |
| Events | 811 | Get event type name | ask(811, N): Returns the name of the type of event N. N is an event ID, from an event list (800-804). | 2 | `SELECT t.name FROM events AS e INNER JOIN event_types AS t ON t.id = e.event_type WHERE e.outcome = 1 AND e.id = [dvalue_1];` |
| Events | 820 | Get acting spacecraft | event_drone(N), or ask(820, N): Returns the ID of the spacecraft whose action caused event N. N is an event ID, from an event list (800-804). | 2 | `SELECT a.drone FROM events AS e INNER JOIN actions AS a ON a.id = e.action WHERE e.outcome = 1 AND e.id = [dvalue_1];` |
| Events | 821 | Get associated protocol | ask(821, N): Returns the ID of the protocol whose action caused event N. N is an event ID, from an event list (800-804). | 2 | `SELECT a.protocol FROM events AS e INNER JOIN actions AS a ON a.id = e.action WHERE e.outcome = 1 AND e.id = [dvalue_1];` |
| Events | 831 | Get affected spacecraft | ask(831, N): Return the ID of the spacecraft affected by event N. N is an event ID, from an event list (800-804). | 2 | `SELECT drone FROM events WHERE outcome=1 AND id=[dvalue_1];` |
| Events | 832 | Get affected resource | ask(832, N): Returns the ID of the resource affected by event N. N is an event ID, from an event list (800-804). | 2 | `SELECT resource FROM events WHERE outcome = 1 AND id = [dvalue_1];` |
| Events | 840 | Get new quantity | ask(840, N): Returns the new quantity set by event N. N is an event ID, from an event list (800-804). | 2 | `SELECT new_quantity FROM events WHERE outcome = 1 AND id = [dvalue_1];` |
| Events | 841 | Get new credits | ask(841, N): Returns the new credits set by event N. N is an event ID, from an event list (800-804). | 2 | `SELECT new_credits AS d_credits FROM events WHERE outcome = 1 AND id = [dvalue_1];` |
| Events | 842 | Get new location | ask(842, N): Returns the new location set by event N. N is an event ID, from an event list (800-804). | 2 | `SELECT new_location FROM events WHERE outcome = 1 AND id = [dvalue_1];` |
| Events | 843 | Get event time | ask(843, N): Returns when event N happened: its game day (day 0 is the world's first) and the even seconds into that day. N is an event ID, from an event list (800-804). | 2 | `SELECT FROM_UNIXTIME(game_t) FROM events WHERE outcome = 1 AND id = [dvalue_1];` |
| Spacecraft | 844 | Get event message | message(N), or ask(844, N): The message of event N, a broadcast (event type 13) from a drone of this drone's company; no row for other events or other companies' messages. N is an event ID, from an event list (800-804). | 2 | `SELECT b.body FROM broadcasts b INNER JOIN events e ON e.action = b.action_id WHERE e.id = [dvalue_1] AND e.event_type = 13 AND b.company_id = (SELECT company_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id=[drone_id]);` |
| Spacecraft | 845 | Event details | event(N), or ask(845, N): Event N in one row: type, type name, acting drone, protocol, affected drone, resource, new quantity, new credits, new location, place, game day, seconds into it, message (your company's only). | 1 | `SELECT e.event_type AS type, t.name AS type_name, IFNULL(x.drone, 0) AS acting, IFNULL(x.protocol, 0) AS protocol, e.drone AS affected, e.resource, e.new_quantity AS quantity, e.new_credits AS credits, e.new_location AS location, e.place, FLOOR((e.game_t - g.epoch) / 86400) AS day, MOD(e.game_t - g.epoch, 86400) AS seconds, (SELECT b.body FROM broadcasts AS b WHERE b.action_id = e.action AND e.event_type = 13 AND b.company_id = (SELECT company_id FROM agents WHERE agent_id=[drone_id]) LIMIT 1) AS message FROM events AS e INNER JOIN event_types AS t ON t.id = e.event_type LEFT JOIN actions AS x ON x.id = e.action INNER JOIN game_clock AS g ON g.id = 1 WHERE e.outcome = 1 AND e.id = [value_1];` |
